West of House
You are standing in an open field west of a white house, with a boarded front door.
There is a small mailbox here.


Starting location: West House
Target score: 80
Model: openai/gpt-4o-mini | Agent type: smart
Maximum steps: 500

LESSONS LEARNED FROM PREVIOUS EPISODES:
Repeat these positive behaviors:
1. Take obviously portable items early and check the score line right after; the first 'take egg' gave a confirmed +5.
2. Read observation text for new nouns and exits, such as the nest, leaves and the broken-item messages, before choosing the next action.
3. Use confirmed room transitions to build a mental map, then deliberately pick exits not yet taken.
4. After a score event, record where and how it happened, then move on to unexplored areas instead of staying nearby.
5. Examine objects in the start area, like the house exterior, to look for ways in before leaving the area.
Avoid these negative behaviors:
1. Do not repeat up/down or north/south room pairs after a pair has returned zero score and no new text.
2. Do not cycle put/take on the same item and container; 'Done.' and 'Taken.' with no score change means stop.
3. Do not drop or throw carried valuables in arbitrary places; the egg and canary read as 'broken' after drops, though the cause is unconfirmed.
4. Do not spend 500 steps on a goal with no progress, such as finding the Living Room, without trying a different approach.
5. Do not keep re-walking the same forest rooms; make a new exit, examine, or open attempt every few moves.
Apply these lessons when relevant; current game feedback takes precedence.

Observation: Up a Tree
You are about 10 feet above the ground nestled among some large branches. The nearest branch above you is above your reach.
Beside you on the branch is a small bird's nest.
In the bird's nest is a large egg encrusted with precious jewels, apparently scavenged by a childless songbird. The egg is covered with fine gold inlay, and ornamented in lapis lazuli and mother-of-pearl. Unlike most eggs, this one is hinged and closed with a delicate looking clasp. The egg appears extremely fragile.

Historian episode review — episode 3
================================================

Review source: llm
Lessons: 5 positive + 5 negative (configured per category: 5)

Strongest: the only confirmed gain was +5 at Up a Tree on 'take egg'. Opening the Behind House window was a sound probe, and the game confirmed it opened. Missed: the Actor never tried entering the window. It walked east instead and never came back to use it. Ineffective: a 30+ step loop with zero score and no new rooms, cycling Clearing, Forest, Forest Path and Up a Tree, plus repeated 'put down/take leaves'. 'Go around' moves landed in different rooms and are unreliable. Untried in the visible trajectory: entering the window, other Behind House exits, other CanyView exits, other exits from Clearing #143. Earlier lessons record the egg reading as broken after drops; this evidence does not confirm the cause, so avoid dropping valuables. Navigation transitions were confirmed; the failure was choice, not mapping. Next: return to the house region, try the opened window, and stop retrying forest pairs.

LESSONS LEARNED
================================
POSITIVES TO REPEAT
1. Take obviously portable items and check the score line immediately; 'take egg' gave a confirmed +5 at Up a Tree.
2. Probe house entrances such as the window; 'open window' gave confirmed text, so follow up by actually trying to enter.
3. Before each move, list exits of the current room not yet taken this episode and choose one of those.
4. After any score event, record the room and action, then head to unvisited areas instead of lingering.
5. Prefer plain compass moves, which confirmed transitions every time, over 'go around' moves that landed in different rooms.

NEGATIVES TO AVOID
1. Do not repeat north/south, up/down or east/west pairs between Forest Path, Clearing and Up a Tree after they return zero score and no new rooms.
2. Do not cycle 'put down leaves' and 'take leaves'; 'Dropped.' and 'Taken.' with no score change happened about ten times.
3. Do not use 'go around forest' or 'go around trees' as exploration; they are unreliable and mostly bounced between known forest rooms.
4. Do not open a window or door and then walk away; after 'open window' succeeds, try entering it before leaving.
5. Do not drop or throw carried valuables casually; the egg read as broken in earlier episodes, so keep them held until a safe place is found.
